# Agents · F · part 5

925 entries, feature-lane to ferni-web-ui. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **feature-lane**: One lane of the plan 36 feature-building session for bonsAI. Builds the feature it is handed inside its own worktree, one feature per commit, every gate green. Hands back code, tests and a report only — it never edits the roadmap, the testing docs or the changelog, never touches the Deck, and never pushes. ([qd313/bonsAI](https://github.com/qd313/bonsAI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-lane.md) · ★3)
- **feature-lead**: Clarify Symfony backend features, obtain approvals, and coordinate architecture, implementation, and independent verification. ([tarlepp/symfony-flex-backend](https://github.com/tarlepp/symfony-flex-backend/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/feature-lead.agent.md) · ★286)
- **feature-lead**: Owns a feature end-to-end. Spawns and coordinates their own team. Reports progress to enterprise-lead. ([Norbert515/vide_cli](https://github.com/Norbert515/vide_cli/blob/HEAD/packages/vide_core/assets/team_framework/agents/feature-lead.md) · ★115)
- **feature-lead**: Leads a feature from design through review by delegating API design, UI work, tests, review, and documentation to specialist subagents. ([CowboyLogic/ai-dev](https://github.com/CowboyLogic/ai-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/feature-lead.agent.md) · ★5)
- **feature-lead**: The Feature Lead owns feature scope definition, acceptance criteria clarity, cross-team coordination for complex features, and ensuring features ship complete. Use this agent for feature scoping, cross-team dependency coordination, or acceptance criteria review. ([Sincebook/Claude-Code-Software-Company](https://github.com/Sincebook/Claude-Code-Software-Company/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-lead.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **feature-lead**: Feature development orchestrator coordinating multi-story features with spec validation, team coordination, and sequential delegation ([vineethsoma/birdmate](https://github.com/vineethsoma/birdmate/blob/HEAD/.github/skills/feature-lead/agents/feature-lead.agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **feature-lead**: Lead technique de TournéeVéto. Planifie une feature, délègue l'implémentation, les tests, le refactoring et l'audit aux agents spécialisés, puis rédige le rapport final. ([AnthonyBenoitDsahr/TourneeVeto2026](https://github.com/AnthonyBenoitDsahr/TourneeVeto2026/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/feature-lead.agent.md))
- **feature-lead**: Planifier une fonctionnalité FrigoMalin, suivre les tâches et créer uniquement un rapport horodaté en fin de cycle, sans modifier le code. ([houssemeddinelassoued/FrigoMalin](https://github.com/houssemeddinelassoued/FrigoMalin/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/feature-lead.agent.md))
- **Feature-Lead**: Chef d'équipe de FrigoMalin : planifie une feature, la découpe en tâches, délègue chaque tâche aux agents spécialistes (Developer, Tester, Refactorer, Security-Reviewer) en sous-agents, décide de la suite selon leurs comptes rendus et rédige un rapport final dans reports/. Ne touche jamais au code. Use when: réaliser une feature de bout en bout, planifier et piloter une fonctionnalité, orchestrer l'équipe, découper en tâches, coordonner développement, tests et audit. ([houssemeddinelassoued/FrigoMalin2026](https://github.com/houssemeddinelassoued/FrigoMalin2026/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/feature-lead.agent.md))
- **feature-lead**: Lead technique de TournéeVéto. Planifie une feature, délègue implémentation, tests, refactoring et audit aux agents spécialisés, décide à partir de leurs comptes rendus et rédige le rapport final. ([houssemeddinelassoued/tourneeveto](https://github.com/houssemeddinelassoued/tourneeveto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-lead.md) · 3 copies)
- **feature-lead**: Feature team lead architect. Designs interfaces, reviews implementations, manages integration, runs final tests. Can write type/interface definitions only. ([Xza85hrf/claude-code-agent-kit](https://github.com/Xza85hrf/claude-code-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-lead.md))
- **feature-lifecycle**: Full per-feature pipeline: implement → testloop → evaluator challenge → coder feedback → document → commit. Each stage gates the next. Evaluator findings feed back to the coder. Nothing is committed until tests are green and the evaluator is satisfied. ([samibs/skillfoundry](https://github.com/samibs/skillfoundry/blob/HEAD/agents/feature-lifecycle.md) · ★12)
- **feature-lifecycle**: Use this agent for end-to-end feature implementation: from spec/planning through code, tests, QA, commit and PR. Trigger when user says 'implement feature', 'new feature', 'build feature', 'feature completa', 'implementar feature', or describes a feature to build from scratch. ([aronpc/ai](https://github.com/aronpc/ai/blob/HEAD/agents/feature-lifecycle.md) · ★1)
- **feature-lifecycle-agent**: Primary software delivery orchestrator. Use when building a new feature end-to-end — from a raw feature request to a verified, production-ready delivery. Coordinates requirement clarification, slice planning, test-first implementation, verification, and release handoff across all architecture layers. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: The user has a new feature request they want implemented. user: "Build me a user notification preferences feature." assistant: "I'll launch the feature-lifecycle-agent to take this from requirements through to verified delivery." &lt;commentary&gt; A new feature request tha… ([hrefel/skills](https://github.com/hrefel/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/feature-development/agents/feature-lifecycle-agent.md))
- **feature-list**: 功能清单列出一项工作要交付的全部功能项：页面、功能、后台任务和第三方对接。规格、开发任务、实现和验收都用功能项编号引用它。清单里记录了每个功能项属于哪个发布批次、由哪些任务实现，便于在复杂或多端项目中清晰追踪范围。 ([hydrz/dev-skills](https://github.com/hydrz/dev-skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/feature-list.md))
- **feature-locator**: Cheap, fast lookup of where a feature or concept lives (entity, handlers, controller, jobs, Angular pages, driver app screens). Use before reading or changing a feature you haven't located yet. Returns paths, not analysis. ([suxrobGM/logistics-app](https://github.com/suxrobGM/logistics-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-locator.md) · ★210)
- **feature-lookup**: Quick-reference for common feature keywords: which package owns a capability, and which backends carry it today. All package names use the `@flighthq/` prefix. ([flighthq/flight](https://github.com/flighthq/flight/blob/HEAD/agents/feature-lookup.md) · ★5)
- **feature-loop-acceptance-verifier**: Verificador de ACEITE independente da aterrissagem (fase 3) da skill feature-loop. Use quando uma fatia (CHG-NNN) foi construída e precisa ser validada contra seus critérios de aceite por um avaliador CEGO ao raciocínio do build — postura adversarial (procurar lacuna, não carimbar), read-only (não conserta nada). Confere cada critério (inclusive o de preservação) contra o código/testes reais, com evidência file:line, e devolve PASS/FAIL/CONCERN + lacunas. É a separação de deveres 'quem decide ≠ quem aprova' aplicada a um processo autônomo. NÃO edita arquivos, NÃO delega construção. ([ruyoutor/agentic-archflow](https://github.com/ruyoutor/agentic-archflow/blob/HEAD/agents/feature-loop-acceptance-verifier.md))
- **feature-maintainer**: 维护任务分类专家。识别业务规则、技术契约、实现修复与纯配置变更，输出影响范围与路由建议。当维护任务需分类时使用。 ([jitawangzi/agent-sop](https://github.com/jitawangzi/agent-sop/blob/HEAD/agents/feature-maintainer.md))

Invoked during the PLAN phase when a story needs to be decomposed into specific implementation tasks with detailed technical specifications. ([suxxes/resin.ai](https://github.com/suxxes/resin.ai/blob/HEAD/orchestrator/agents/feature-manager.md) · ★2)

Use it to capture new requests as issues, refine and prioritise them, write implementation plans, and turn chosen features into work orders for the local coder and review its code, ready for the next build. - **feature-memory**: Maintains ai-docs/README.md — the shared code map every session and agent loads. Invoke when: - A feature PASSED review — final harness step (Feature Mode) - The code map drifted from disk and needs a full rebuild (Project Scan) Examples: - "Update memory for text-pack-translation" → adds feature, rebuilds graph - "Rescan project memory" → full rebuild, features list preserved ([app-builders-club/mvp-builder](https://github.com/app-builders-club/mvp-builder/blob/HEAD/agents/feature-memory.md) · ★13)

- **fence-latency-2026-11**: - **観測日**: 2026-11-12 - **問い**: Fence（既存の囲いの CLI）を、pi の `shellPath` の先に置くと、bash・`bg_run`・`!`・子エージェントを包めるか。1 コマンドごとの遅さは何が原因で、避けられるか。 - **結論**: 包める。GPU・秘密の非表示・`/tmp` の分離・通信の許可リスト・標準のキャッシュも、設定で通った。遅さは、WSL の `/mnt` 配下の `PATH` 要素の参照（約 0.77 秒）と、作業ディレクトリのファイル数に比例する走査が主因。ラッパーで `PATH` から `/mnt` を外すと、1 コマンド約 1.0 秒が約 0.25 秒になる（実測）。公式は「コマンドごとに包む使い方より、エージェント全体を 1 回包む使い方」を勧めており、常駐・再利用の仕組みは無い。 ([applejxd/dotfiles](https://github.com/applejxd/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/research/agents/fence-latency-2026-11.md))
- **fence-reviewer**: Use to attack finished work before it is trusted — a change to src/, a decision record before it is accepted, a work item about to be marked done, a release candidate. Its job is to refute, not to confirm; it reports findings with evidence and hands back. Do NOT use it to fix what it finds. ([dotcarls/fence.js](https://github.com/dotcarls/fence.js/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fence-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **fence-scribe**: Use for documentation and tracker hygiene — writing or revising a document under docs/, keeping README/MIGRATING/CHANGELOG accurate, creating or updating work items and their acceptance criteria, refreshing the checkpoint, wiring cross-references, or making an unreachable document reachable. Do NOT use it to decide anything or to change code. ([dotcarls/fence.js](https://github.com/dotcarls/fence.js/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fence-scribe.md) · ★1)
